Suite 1549 → 1552 tests, 43676 → 43763 assertions, 2 skipped unchanged. The remaining six findings are ADJUDICATED, NOT FIXED — each was reproduced and is real; they sit on other surfaces or subsystems than the round's three and are next-round material with their fix shapes named. Do not re-derive them; re-open only to fix:

- **A case-variant 'Plugin Name:' header ships a second WordPress-visible plugin (HIGH)**: wp_connectors_find_main_plugin_files() probes strpos($head, 'Plugin Name:') byte-exactly (and its root glob is exact-case '*.php') while WordPress's get_file_data() matches header names case-insensitively — and the repo's OWN header pattern (wp_connectors_plugin_header_pattern()) is /mi: one vocabulary, two folds, the discovery one exact. Reproduced: a root rogue.php docblock saying 'plugin name: Rogue' beside the real main file ships BOTH at exit 0 with inspection green; WordPress lists the zip as two plugins — the exact ambiguity wp_connectors_main_file_violations() exists to prevent. Fix shape: fold the discovery probe (and the glob's extension case) at that one owner, pinned over the real connectors/zai tree. Deferred: the fold changes which files the exactly-one-main-file gate counts — its own pinning round.
- **A case-variant build.json FILENAME silently means no-embed (HIGH)**: the config seam resolves $pluginDir.'/build.json' byte-exactly, so 'Build.json' builds a library-less zip at exit 0 with inspection green (reproduced; class_exists false through the shipped autoloader after extraction) — the t31-r4-6 silent-no-embed class one level up from the key level (case-variant KEYS already refuse loudly, verified). Since t31-r6-3 the collector also excludes 'Build.json' as a dev entry, so the misfiled config disappears from the ship too. Fix shape: a case-variant 'build.json' spelling in the plugin root REFUSES loudly (the K2 typo-fence pattern) instead of silently no-embedding. Deferred: a new refusal path on the config seam. **r13 forward**: a DANGLING SYMLINK at build.json is the same silent-no-embed shape (is_file() follows the link and reads false — no embed, no refusal); that round's fix owns both spellings (the is_link() refusal folds in here).
- **The secret scanner prunes the very subtree the embed ships by design (HIGH — the sweep's most security-relevant finding)**: wp_connectors_scan_paths() prunes {'.git','vendor','node_modules','dist','tools','.phpunit.cache'} segments, while the inspector exempts <slug>/src/Shared/ from the dev-entry vocabulary precisely because shared/src has no exclusion concepts (t31-r5-5 names shared/src/tools/ as the blessed example). Intersection = a no-scan zone: a live-shaped AWS key in shared/src/tools/Keys.php embeds, builds, passes inspection AND the repo scan (reproduced; the identical file at shared/src/Keys.php rejects). Fix shape: the prune is a dev-tree concept — the scan of the embedded subtree (or of any shipped artifact) must not prune inside it. Deferred: secret-scanner.php is a subsystem shared by the repo scan and the inspector; the pruning doctrine is its own round. **CLOSED as t31-r12-3** — the named round came: the prune is a parameter (repo scans keep it, artifact scans run unpruned), and the src/Shared exemption composes as classification-only.
- **The zip-wide case-duplicate entry set is undetected (MEDIUM)**: a plugin owning 'Assets/logo.png' AND 'assets/logo.png' ships both with inspection green; on a folding extraction target one silently overwrites the other (reproduced). Subsumes the file-vs-directory residual named at the t31-r6-1 fence (a case-folding plugin DIRECTORY beside an injected/generated file — 'license/notes.txt' beside the injected LICENSE — ships and fails extraction as a file-vs-directory conflict): both fences compare collected FILE entries only. Fix shape: ONE case-fold collision fence over the FINAL entry set AND its directory prefixes at the sort, mirroring r5-16 — closes both shapes together. Deferred: a new fence class, its own battery rows. **PARTIALLY CONSUMED as t31-r12-15** — the INSPECTOR half is fenced (case-fold duplicate entry names now reject at inspection, making the deferred builder-side defect VISIBLE as build-green/inspect-red instead of silently green); the BUILDER-side fence over the collected entry set and its directory prefixes (the file-vs-directory residual included) remains this line's deferred scope.
- **A backslash filename splits the one verdict (MEDIUM)**: 'weird\name.php' (legal on ext4) ships through collectFiles at exit 0 while the inspector rejects any '\' entry — build-green/inspect-red on the same artifact (reproduced), the one-verdict doctrine violated in the opposite direction from t31-r5-10's. Fix shape: collectFiles refuses '\' in relative names (the inspector's rule, at the builder's collector). Not case-sensitivity; adjacent.
- **A '.. '-spelled directory ships past the traversal check (MEDIUM)**: the inspector's in_array('..', $parts, true) is byte-exact; a directory literally named '.. ' ships (reproduced) and folds to '..' on Win32-normalizing targets — the host-traversal outcome the check exists to prevent. Fix shape: the traversal check strips each segment's trailing edge junk (the t31-r6-4/5 junk-class owner) before judging — the same normalization the dev-entry fold already rides.
- **Theoretical/observed-not-defects (do not re-flag without a real producer)**: strcasecmp is ASCII-only, so a Kelvin-sign 'PAC<U+212A>AGE.JSON' is not a dev entry — unicode default-caseless folding on APFS could fold it, but the producer is exotic and target behavior is unverifiable here; a plugin 'LICENSE ' (trailing space) ships beside the injected LICENSE with the plugin's copy winning on folding targets — the doctrine's intended outcome, cosmetic on case-sensitive hosts; the scanner's extension allowlist misses .php5/.inc/.phtml in every channel (a name-class gap, not case); case-variant connector slugs ('zai' + 'Zai') both publish on a case-sensitive dist/ — local build output only.

## M3 / Task 3.1 fix round t31-r4 (branch feat/m3-task31-contracts, 2026-09-14)

Fix round over round-4's 13 counted findings, executed as TWO structural class-kills (K1: the rewrite postcondition as a total scan; K2: the config seam as closed-schema validation) plus eleven single-finding commits — the round's own design mandate, per-spelling regex patching being exhausted (the same seams had been closed "one spelling away" twice). 15 commits t31-r4-K1/K2/3/7/8/9/10/11/12/13/14, then a two-lens verifier pass (independent correctness + security agents over the whole round diff; every confirmed finding adversarially re-driven by the implementer before fixing). Suite 1521 → 1538 tests, 43256 → 43498 assertions, 2 skipped unchanged; green in default and random order throughout.

- **K1 honest boundary — SPLIT-COMPOSED namespace spellings are CONSCIOUSLY ACCEPTED (verifier, both lenses)**: a reference whose RUNTIME VALUE is `Deicod\WpConnectors\Shared` but whose BYTES never spell it contiguously — concatenated pieces (`'Deicod\' . 'WpConnectors\' . 'Shared\Clock'`), interpolation (`"…\{$mid}\…"`), double-escaped string literals (`"Deicod\\WpConnectors\\Shared"` — file bytes carry two backslashes), and docblock ` * `-continuation breaks — passes the survivor scan, the sweep gate, and ships (all reproduced by the security lens). The pieces are ordinary string fragments; only their runtime value names the namespace, and a spelling-level scan cannot see values. No shared source composes the namespace dynamically today (the whole tree rides plain use statements), and every CODE-position split the lens tried is a PHP parse error (`php -l` refuses whitespace/comment-interrupted names). Re-open ONLY if a shared source ever resolves the namespace dynamically (`new $name`, `class_exists($composed)`, callable maps) — the fix shape is a no-dynamic-class-resolution gate over shared/src, never a wider spelling pattern.
- **A slug literally 'shared' refuses its own embed — fail-closed, accepted (verifier, correctness lens)**: `namespaceSuffixFromSlug('shared')` derives the suffix `Shared`, so the rewritten target `…\WpConnectors\Shared\Shared` trips the survivor scan and every shared source refuses. The tree refuses loudly at the seam (before any filesystem mutation); a plugin whose slug collides with the architecture's own `Shared` segment is pathological and the refusal names the file. Re-open only if such a plugin is ever actually wanted (then the embed wants its own namespace segment choice, not a scan widening).
- **A group-use member ALIASED exactly 'Shared' refuses — fail-loud, pinned (verifier, security lens)**: `use Deicod\WpConnectors\{Clock as Shared};` imports the DIFFERENT namespace `…\WpConnectors\Clock`, but the total scan cannot distinguish the alias's member-boundary position from the namespace segment, so the build refuses (pinned as a refusal in the spelling battery; the rewriter docblock states the contract). Do not flag as a false positive to fix by narrowing the scan — the narrowing reopens the t31-r4-4 class; rename the alias.
- **The WP-reach curation doctrine is EXPLICIT now (t31-r4-11's adjudicated half)**: the closed-stem vocabulary is spelling-curated, per the t31-r2-7 clock/env doctrine — a spelling joins the list when someone writes one (the round added the obviously-conditional/admin surface a source could plausibly reach for: is_admin, get_bloginfo, is_user_logged_in, get_locale; `wp_*` needs no closed stem, the open class owns it). Unlisted plain functions (add_meta_box, user_can, …) are the SAME posture, not misses: do not re-flag unlisted names as defects — flag them as curation proposals, or write the spelling and let the mutation discipline add it. The MECHANISM (stem boundaries matching `_`-suffixed twins, letter-aware lookahead) is closed and exhaustively probed (52 stems × twin/plain/split).
- **The raw-port rejection is ADJUDICATED-FIXED with a recorded narrowing (t31-r4-12)**: parse_url's silent truncation (`:443x` → 443) diverged from the URL string the caller holds — the fix validates the raw authority's port substring as fully digits. TWO narrowings ride the adjudication, both deliberate: the EMPTY port (`https://host:/`) now rejects (RFC 3986 permits it; parse_url ignored it) because the built authority drops the colon while the raw string carries it — the same divergence class; and leading-zero ports accept, spelled by their int value. Re-open the empty-port clause only with a real provider endpoint spelled that way (then accept-and-normalize is the supersession).
- **The manifest is a RECORD, not an inventory (t31-r4-8 residual, verifier-noted)**: entries survive partial rebuilds and failed runs whole, and no run destroys an entry it did not build — but a zip deleted out-of-band leaves its entry behind (a subsequent full rebuild does not reconcile it; the pre-round wipe did, at the cost of the two pinned defects). The build.php comment states this honestly. Re-open only if the manifest ever feeds an inventory-consuming tool (then a reconcile-on-full-run flag is the fix, never the pre-run wipe).
- **K2 inert-key scope (verifier, correctness lens)**: `namespace_suffix` is type+segment validated whenever PRESENT (embed on or off), but the autoloader cross-check runs only when embedding — a mismatched-but-inert suffix beside `embed_shared: false` builds. Intrinsic validity and use-validity are different contracts; the key is unused when the embed is off. Re-open only if a consumer ever reads namespace_suffix outside the embed path.
- **Hardlinks are OUT OF the no-symlinks doctrine (verifier, security lens)**: a hardlink is a real directory entry; the dev autoloader and the build resolve it identically, so no loads-in-dev/misses-the-zip divergence exists and no link boundary is crossed (the harm model r4-7/16 close). Do not propose a hardlink gate without a demonstrated divergence.
- **The `.PHP` extension consumers are ALL on the one owner now, with one fail-closed straggler (t31-r4-9/18)**: collect (vocabulary), strip (PSR-4 stem), classify (self-containment, unused-import), the inspector's syntax loop, and the lint gate ride `wp_connectors_is_php_source()`. Main-file discovery's `glob('*.php')` remains case-SENSITIVE — a `.PHP`-spelled main file is refused as "no main plugin file" (fail-closed, correct direction); re-open only if that refusal's message ever misleads.
- **Verifier-pass verdicts (both lenses, every claim empirically re-driven)**: all 11 fix-claims HELD — highlights: the pre-round code A/B'd through scratch scaffolds for every claimed repro (group-use survival, multiline survival at exit 0, the `'Array'` suffix build, the unloadable custom suffix, `:443x` truncation, raw-0x85 construction with json_encode false); the group-use rewrite battery extended to 10 legal member shapes (all outputs `php -l` clean) with case-variant/nested/kind-case refusals; the survivor scan's soundness re-driven over the real tree for three suffix shapes; a REAL PCRE abort driven through the use-rewrite (300k-segment subject → "use-statement rewrite aborted (PCRE)"); a real failing close() driven at the seam with libzip's own warning asserted as evidence and the second-close ValueError confirmed; the closed schema attacked with homoglyph/unicode-escaped/trailing-space keys (all refused); the URL battery attacked with userinfo/IPv6/backslash/scheme-case/fragment tricks (all rejected, accepted URLs always json-encodable). - **Case-variant namespace spellings survive the rewrite silently — PRE-EXISTING posture, CONSCIOUSLY ACCEPTED** (t31-r3-2 verifier note): PHP namespaces are case-INsensitive, so 'use deicod\wpconnectors\shared\Clock;' resolves to the shared namespace at runtime while matching neither the rewrite patterns (exact-case) nor the postcondition's byte probe nor the architecture sweep's whitelist — it rewrites to nothing and ships pointing at a namespace the rewrite otherwise renamed. - **The token-set port commits generation-checked (t31-r1-13, the DECIDE+IMPLEMENT scope note)**: `save(provider, grant, expected_generation): bool` is a compare-and-set against the PERSISTED generation — Task 3.3's "a fencing generation checked before every refresh commit", widened into the port BEFORE 3.2/3.3 pin the three-method shape. `EXPECT_NO_GRANT = -1` names the absent precondition (the connect/exchange first install); a false return is a fence verdict (the stale writer reloads and discards its tokens), never an error. TWO VERIFIER NOTES, both deliberate non-promises: the CAS checks expectation EQUALITY, not monotonic advance (an expected-5 commit of a generation-1 grant returns true — `with_generation()` enforces forward-only on the caller side, and 3.3's coordinator is the only writer that constructs new generations; do not re-flag without a second writer), and a 3.2 FORWARD NOTE: `update_option()` alone is NOT a CAS (its false conflates unchanged-value with failure) — the envelope implementation needs a guarded single-statement UPDATE or equivalent to uphold the documented invariant.
- **Token-set reads are FORWARD-TOLERANT, the envelope owns versioning (t31-r1-14, the DECIDE scope note)**: the five modelled keys stay required and strictly validated; keys this version does not model are IGNORED (Task 4.4's id-token facts member loads on the older reader instead of fail-closing every stored grant into a forced re-connect). An inner version field was considered and REJECTED — the envelope's versioned-encryption invariant is the single format-version authority; a second one inside the payload buys nothing. Honest cost, documented at both ends and pinned: a load → save round trip through an older reader DROPS the unmodelled keys. Do not re-flag the dropped keys as data loss or propose the inner version field again.

## M3 / Task 3.1 review round (branch feat/m3-task31-contracts, 2026-09-13)

Two-lens round (four dimension reviewers + independent adversarial verification) over the six Task-3.1 commits; 8 findings raised, 4 CONFIRMED (fixed as 9c41baa — CRLF header-line injection rejected at both VO constructors, `\z` anchors on the method-token and PKCE verifier patterns, `with_state(Revoked)` rejected so no un-advanced tombstone is mintable — and ec2ba0d, the sweep's dead `doing_it_wrong` entry fixed to `_doing_it_wrong` plus the per-site/URL vocabulary added, mutation-batteried). The 4 refutations, each verifier-reproduced before refutation:

- **The PKCE CSRF `state` member is NOT part of the Task-3.1 flow shapes** (consciously deferred): SPEC §4.3 fixes `state` generation/validation (paste-back CSRF), but the work order's item 8 pins only "code_verifier/challenge + pending state per user" — the `state` member's charset, length, and storage home are not fixed by SPEC or plan, so the neutral `PendingAuthorization`/`PkceCodePair` stay without it; the member lands with the plugin flow implementation (Task 3.6 / M6) where SPEC's flow facts become binding. Re-open only with a plan/order clause assigning the member to the shared contracts.
- **parse_url's lenient host charset is NOT a Task-3.1 violation** (verifier: "empirically half-confirmed, refuted as a violation"): `Url::parse_validated()` accepts hosts with spaces/tabs (php -r reproduced, the bytes reach `redacted_url()`), but constructor validation's job here is scheme/host/port shape, not RFC 3986 host-charset enforcement; the debug forms carry no credentials in the host position and no production caller exists yet. Below-the-bar; re-open if a caller ever forwards the authority unfiltered to a wire request.
- **`with_state()` on a token-BEARING grant was already correctly rejected** — the confirmation that mattered was the token-LESS path (9c41baa); the token-bearing rejection predates the round and its pin (`UseRevokeInstead`) was green throughout.
- **The five-name sensitive-header allowlist breadth is NOT a defect** (verifier: "empirically accurate, refuted as a violation"): exotic credential-bearing spellings (`X-Goog-Api-Key`, `Private-Token`, a leading-space name) render verbatim, but the VOs' documented redaction contract names exactly three vectors — Authorization header, token-bearing URL, token-bearing body — all masked/omitted by construction, the providers' token channel is `Authorization`, and non-standard header redaction is the Task-3.7 catalog's job. Expanding the allowlist is whack-a-mole; re-open only with a live provider capture sending credentials in an unmapped header.

Steady state: the healthy configured path pays exactly ONE resolution (unavoidable — the binding compare needs the current cache key) and the no-state path pays ZERO; an eagerly-threaded resolution would ADD cost to the no-state path, so threading is incorrect, not merely unprofitable. - **glm36 round decisions** (fix round over the 7 surviving findings of code-review round 36 — /code-review max, ledger-filtered: 6 of the 13 emitted findings dropped as ledger-covered re-flags at the caller — the glm34-5/proph absent-member usage tolerance (a message_delta without usage consolidating output_tokens 0), glm18's known by-reference-CALLEE scanner boundary, glm26-2's ascending-interleave tolerance (named in the proph allow-list), glm18's goto backward edge (phpcs-gated), the GLM2 #8 deferred duplicate-settings-errors neighborhood (per glm31's precedent, deferred items are closed by scope decision), and glm31-9's JsonShape forwarder design; 7 fix commits glm36-1..7, then a two-lens verifier pass — independent correctness + security agents over the full round diff — whose FIVE empirically-confirmed scanner laundering channels and one probe silent-default falsified or extended the round's own claims and are fixed as glm36-8/9, with one doc-drift class fixed as glm36-10):
  - **Every destructuring and dynamic write spelling refuses the include proof on BOTH write paths** (glm36-1, falsified in its first form and completed by glm36-8 — the verifier round's centerpiece): glm36-1 gated the PHP 7.1+ square spelling beside the map path's list() refusal and ported both to the plain-variable collector, but its statement-level anchor spared a bracket glued to '(' or a keyword ('([$f] = …)', 'if ([$f] = …)', 'return[$f] = …', 'foo([$f] = …)' — all empirically PWNED at HEAD), its '[^)]*' list span could not cross a nested paren ('list( list($a), $f )', 'list( ($a), $f )'), the foreach VALUE bindings ('as [$f]', "as ['k' => $f]", 'as $k => [$f]', 'as &$f') were invisible to both paths pre-round AND post-glm36-1 (the header's bracket group ends ')' or ',', never '='), variable-variable writes ('$$name', "{'f'}" braced) never spell the target textually (the masked view blanks the braced name), and the round's own '[^;]*' spans are quadratic — a ~4 KB bracket-run BURNER statement exhausts pcre.backtrack_limit, preg_match() returns FALSE, and the old truthy reads treated the abort as "no match," spending the call's budget before the real write was examined. glm36-8 closes all five: the anchor is GONE (a bracket group an assignment follows can only re-bind what it names), the list span is '[^;]*', a header-bounded foreach pattern (paren-bounded after 'as', so the SOURCE position and loop-body brackets never trip it) refuses bracket bindings on both paths, the alias pattern gains its 'as &' twin, one '$$'/'${' anywhere in the visible regions refuses every proof in the file (dynamic naming defeats static proof wholesale — glm18-18's rule), and EVERY refusal reads '0 !== preg_match(...)' with both preg_match_all walks refusing on FALSE — a PCRE abort is a REFUSAL, never a no-match. Documented over-approximations, all safe-direction (glm29-3's doctrine): an element WRITE keyed by the variable ('$rows[$map] = 1', a read of the variable as index) now refuses (the anchor that spared it was the anchor-bypass hole), a pure list() source read ('list($a) = $map') refuses, and any file using variable-variables at all loses its proofs. Word boundaries after the variable end the '$mapx' false-refusal class. 13 new laundering fixtures + the burner-abort pin (a pure comparison that must flag through the guard alone); the real tree stays clean under composer conventions.
  - **stop_sequence's non-string degrade is the LEDGERED envelope-metadata tolerance, stated honestly** (glm36-2, the round's first boundary adjudication): a present-but-non-string stop_sequence latches nothing and flags nothing on the stream (key-present null in additionalData) while the byte-identical non-streaming body's vendor pass-through carries the value verbatim — reproduced end-to-end on both channels by the round AND both verifier lenses (every SCHEMA-LEGAL input — string, empty string, explicit null — agrees on both channels; the divergence exists only for values the Messages schema types string|null). Accepted, not latch-and-flag: the member is non-load-bearing on both channels (zero plugin consumers), the stream side is pinned by the proph harness's allow-list entry, and the class is GLM1 #9's (message_start's model degrades identically, ledgered as non-load-bearing metadata) — re-opening proph's adjudication would need new evidence and the divergence predates it. The one DEFECT was the comments: both the latch site and aggregated()'s header claimed "envelope parity" the channels do not deliver on non-legal inputs — rewritten to the real contract, and a behavioral pin asserts the accepted divergence in both directions (stream null + unflagged success; body verbatim). Residual, ledgered (correctness lens): an ABSENT member leaves the stream's key present-null while the body omits the key — a pre-existing shape asymmetry outside the adjudicated non-string claim.

- **proph round decisions** (NOT a review round — test infrastructure: the mutation-invariant property harness for both SSE aggregators, proph-1 702330d + the verifier-round hardening proph-2 7cf37e9, closing the silent-divergence/silent-loss class the loop chased shape-by-shape — glm23-6, glm28-2, glm31-1, glm31-2, glm33-1, glm34-1 were all members — as an invariant over the mutation space; ZERO production defects found: the class as pinned at HEAD holds under every generated mutation):
  - **The invariant** (tests/SseAggregatorMutationPropertyTest.php): for every (well-formed corpus stream × mutation operator × site), the aggregation is exactly one of CLEAN (identical output and flags), FLAGGED (output differs and a corruption flag rose — or a Throwable escaped, which FAILs), or TOLERATED (output differs, no flag, and the observation matches an EXPLICITLY allow-listed class with a ledger citation). NEVER a diff without flag and entry, never an escaping Error/TypeError. The allow-list is enumerated, not discovered — an unmatched observation fails with seed, operator, and reproducing stream; widening requires adjudication here. The payload comparator is TYPE-STRICT and MAP-ORDER-INSENSITIVE (maps by member set, lists in order — content order is semantic, key order is not; proph-2: the first form's loose != equated null/false/[] and classified real null-family flips CLEAN, verifier-reproduced 9x per 60k).
  - **The operator table** (18 operators, ledger-history mapped): cut-data-json / cut-data-only-frame / byte-inject-nul / byte-split-utf8 / truncate-stream-mid-frame → glm23-6 and glm34-1 (undecodable payloads, the data-only corner included); drop-data-line → glm23-1 (the cut declaration); drop-event-line → glm23-1/glm33-1 (the cut carrier); drop-type-member → glm33-1 (the typeless payload, frame-level and delta-level); member-wrong-type / member-absent / member-empty-string → glm26-3, glm28-2, glm29-4, glm31-1, glm31-2, GLM7 #8 (every member a reader touches); duplicate-frame / swap-frames → glm16-2, glm26-2; partial-then-valid → the proxy-cut continuation; unknown-enum / unknown-event-agreed → glm33-4, GLM7 #18, GLM1 #15; usage-corrupt → glm31-1, Codex R15 #1; drop-frame → the wire-integrity class. Coverage and non-vacuity gates fail loudly if an operator starves or a channel goes unwitnessed.
  - **The wire-integrity tolerance class** (both surfaces, the generic entries' foundation): SSE carries no per-frame sequencing or checksum field, so a vanished, duplicated, or reordered WHOLE frame is indistinguishable from a stream that arrived that way — the aggregators' charter is visible in-band damage (frames are merged "as received"). The Anthropic twin still flags lifecycle-frame drops/reorderings through its required serialized lifecycle (verifier-probed: 20 hand-built lifecycle damage shapes all flag PAST the generic entries — the class tolerates exactly the undetectable: same-block delta loss/dup/reorder, glm26-2's ascending-interleave, glm16-2's duplicate terminal). Entry-granularity variants (a vanished choices/tool_calls element) ride the same class.
  - **Proph-adjudicated tolerances** (each surfaced by the harness itself during bring-up, zero production defects): the GLM9 #1/glm33-4/GLM8 #4 stop_reason latch (ANY string — '' included — and the schema's explicit null latch as received; unmapped values reject typed at finish_reason_for()); the GLM4 #11/Codex R15 #1 usage classes (absent members default-zero, valid non-negative-int VALUES are transparent — the validator judges type and non-negativity, never counts); the GLM12 #9/glm34-5 empty shapes ({} usage is the null-usage normalization gateway form; an empty message object is the composite of individually documented absent-member tolerances); the GLM1 #15/glm13-4 unknown-type drops (the EMPTY string names no type — the streamed drop and the started-block delta tolerance apply verbatim); the GLM7 #1/GLM10 #13 index-value merge identity (the index TYPE is corruption-judged, the value is the wire's own addressing — sparse/non-zero-starting are the documented legitimate shapes); and the GLM5 #3 associative {} / [] collapse (an array-shaped delta/function/tool_calls/choices member with no recognizable members is the empty-chunk skip, not corruption — a NON-empty list of non-entries still flags).
  - **Cross-surface parity, pinned**: shared corruption shapes (undecodable data-only frames, decodable scalars, unknown agreed names, corrupt-only usage, non-string roles, duplicated terminals) must earn matching verdict classes on both twins; the ONE encoded divergence is the terminal drop — the Anthropic wire REQUIRES the message_start/message_delta/message_stop lifecycle (every aggregated() null path flags, Codex R8 #3/GLM7 #5/Codex R16 #1/glm35-1) while the OpenAI [DONE] is a terminal marker whose absence is undetectable (the choices-emptiness gate glm26-11; GLM5 #7).
  - **proph verifier-pass decisions** (two independent lenses over proph-1; all findings harness-side, fixed as proph-2 — no production change): the allow-list is SOUND against over-breadth (every generic entry probed with hand-built lifecycle shapes; the index entries cannot mask a merge collision — the operator's fixed value 5 against corpus indexes 0/1 cannot construct the collision shape, and the collision itself is the GLM10 #13 verbatim-travel pin anyway; anthropic corrupt usage kinds ALL flag through the shared validator, only valid-int value changes tolerate); the gates are structurally sound (CASES=1/abc/garbage go RED at the coverage gate; CASES=0 falls back to the FULL default — never a thinner run); determinism verified (per-surface reseeds, zero other mt_rand consumers in the repo, random-order suite green); 7.4-compat and phpcs clean. Fixed: the loose comparator, the mislabeled drop-type probe target ('data-only.0.type' left its entry unreachable), the DEAD decodable-scalar cut entry (removed with the proof: a cut damaging a CONTRIBUTING frame always leaves undecodable JSON and a cut of a non-contributing scalar changes nothing — decodable-scalar SKIP frames joined the corpora instead), the missing CASES ceiling (250k; a leaked soak var can no longer hang the check pipeline), and the seed clamp's float-cast overflow ((float)(PHP_INT_MAX-2) rounds UP to 2^63 — the +1 offset left the int domain and mt_srand threw; digit-length-exact now, SEED=0 honored). Residual, ledgered (security lens, LOW): the effective env CASES/SEED are visible only through the assertion count and failure reports — never printed on green runs — and a sub-default green run (roughly the 60–1999 band) is thinner without a recorded note; the coverage gate still forces ≥18 firing operators, and the fixed-seed parity battery runs regardless.
  - **Numbers**: default 2000 cases/surface rides composer check (~0.6 s of a 6.2 s suite; 1264 tests, 42032 assertions, green in default AND random order); soaks 20k and 50k per surface green TWICE (pre- and post-proph-2; 100k total mutations, 300040 assertions, ~17 s each); alternate seeds (0, 15- and 20-digit clamp probes, three fixed) green; hostile CASES probes red-or-full-default as designed.

- **glm35 round decisions** (fix round over the 10 findings of code-review round 35 — /code-review max, ledger-filtered: ZERO novel correctness defects, the round's one candidate correctness flag struck as a glm26-2 re-flag — the ledgered ascending-stops-over-interleaved-starts pre-existing tolerance, re-derived without new evidence; 10 fix commits glm35-1..10, then a two-lens verifier pass whose one confirmed finding is fixed as glm35-11):
  - **The zai_anthropic stream parse rides the malformed-event channel ALONE** (glm35-1): every aggregated() null return raises the flag first — the three null paths (no message_start, no stop_reason-bearing message_delta, no message_stop) each set malformed_event before returning, and the model checks the flag AFTER calling aggregated() — so the former separate 'No usable message event was received.' branch below the flag check was unreachable dead code on this wire (its required message_start/message_delta/message_stop lifecycle makes every unusable stream a flagged truncation — Codex R8 #3, GLM7 #5, Codex R16 #1). Deleted, the invariant stated at the malformed throw and the aggregator's @return, and source-pinned absent. The zai twin's null channel stays LIVE by design (an OpenAI choices-less stream is unusable without being malformed, a shape this wire cannot produce) — do not copy the branch back from the twin.
  - **Every canonical SSE segment a builder can emit rides the builder** (glm35-2, completing the glm34-10 conversion the round under-counted): 28 hand-spelled message_delta+message_stop tails, 4 content_block_stop frames, 3 message_start frames — each byte-verified against the builder's runtime output BEFORE the swap through a layout-independent matcher (the four source lines could sit in any junction arrangement, trailing-noise continuations included). Deliberately left hand-spelled: malformed fixtures whose damage IS the tail (stop_reason:5), canonical tails the builder cannot emit byte-identically (input-side usage members, usage-less deltas), the seven array-idiom compositions, and every delta-without-stop truncation fixture. Verifier: all removed literals collapse to six distinct shapes, byte-identical to the builders at the exact tuples used (10/10, key order and separators included).
  - **The protocol-trait pin derives its class set by sweep** (glm35-3): the pin hand-listed the three SDK-interfaced files, so the fourth class it exists for (embeddings/count-tokens) could never be seen — and the omission fails open in production (fallback_authentication()'s base default is plain ApiKey auth, which z.ai accepts). The set is DERIVED now: every class under connectors/zai/src implementing the SDK's own WithRequestAuthenticationInterface (directly or through any parent — class_implements includes parent interfaces) under this surface's ZaiAnthropic naming convention must compose SpeaksAnthropicMessagesProtocol (checked through the reflection trait chain, not source text), supply the raw hook, and carry no bespoke wrap override. A >=3 lower bound proves the sweep sees the real tree; a fourth class enters automatically. The reflection assertions are strictly stronger than the old source-substring checks (a bare `use` import satisfied those). Residuals, ledgered (verifier, both pre-existing pin shapes, not regressions): the override-forbidding regexes pin the two HISTORICAL wrap spellings — a novel wrap-free getRequestAuthentication() override escapes the lexical pin (the three current classes are backstopped behaviorally; a fourth class needs its behavioral pin on arrival), and the sweep's class gate is the surface's naming convention (a class under another prefix is invisible — the convention every class of the surface follows, stated in the pin's comment).
  - **The body parse's member rule rides the content-block table's BLOCK half** (glm35-4 + glm35-11, the verifier round's one confirmed finding): the text/thinking arms' hand-rolled isset+is_string probes are ONE hoisted guard reading AnthropicContentBlocks::STRING_CONTENT_MEMBERS — the map's own contract ("every consumer") — with the message derived from the closed constant keys, byte-identical to the per-arm spellings; tool_use keeps its identity/input arms, and the lockstep pin now asserts the model rides the table, forbids every hand-rolled isset spelling, and drives a behavioral member battery (absent/non-string/foreign-string member per entry, non-vacuous against MAPPED_TYPES). The verifier found the first form's lookup UNSCOPED: the table also carries the two DELTA names (the aggregator's delta arms read the same map in their own context), so a body content block TYPED as a delta name answered 'A text_delta block is missing its text member.' where the pre-round parse and the commit's own claim gave the switch's unsupported-type rejection — message-only (same class, 'content' channel, reject verdict on every input; a delta-named block WITH its member still hit the unsupported arm both ways). glm35-11 scopes the lookup to MAPPED_TYPES (the block half) and adds the delta-named-block shapes to the battery (absent member, and the member the unscoped guard would read PRESENT and string — derived from the table's non-mapped keys so a restructure cannot run vacuous; mutation-tested: the unscoped form fails with the finding's exact message).
  - **The map memo stores no digest nothing reads** (glm35-5): glm26-6 replaced the digest compare with a strict stored-list compare but left the md5 computed and stored on every rebuild — zero readers in src/tests/bin, a whole-list hash per catalog change for nothing, and a docblock still calling it "the entry's identity". The key, the md5, and the shape clause are gone; the class, orchestrator, and both directory docblocks state the strict-compare contract in present tense (history narration kept as history); the corrupt-entry test keeps its failOnWarning purpose (the string-only filter the warning once rode still runs). Security lens: no DoS surface (400-deep and 1MiB payloads and 20k-frame floods all within milliseconds of pre-round, the flagging path not slower than the drop path; 50k pending declarations constant-memory), no value leakage (the fixed rejection message never carries argument data — pre-round the same value actually SHIPPED), no untyped-crash channel (throwing/resource-holding serializers behave identically pre/post; the caller-detonation class is ledgered trust-boundary), no error-channel inflation (flag_corrupt_event(null) cannot set has_error() or record an availability verdict), and no new file/network/eval constructs or credential material. Full suite green in default and random order (1254 tests, 29926 assertions, 2 skipped — the live-key gates); the one first-run BuildArtifactsTest zip-unlink error reproduced green in isolation and on re-run (the ledgered glm31 dist-artifact environmental conditional; the diff touches no build tooling).

- **glm32 round decisions** (fix round over the 2 surviving cleanup findings of code-review round 32 — /code-review max, ledger-filtered: the review's 10 finder angles produced 20 candidates, its own verification refuted 3, the gap sweep was empty, and triage dropped 13 of the 15 emitted findings as ledgered re-flags or in-code documented decisions — zero correctness defects; five angles came back completely clean):
  - **apply_delta()'s text/thinking arms append unconditionally** (glm32-1, the glm24-5 dead-conjunct class completing glm15-21): the text_delta/thinking_delta arms re-checked isset()+is_string() on the very member dispatch_event()'s has_string_content_member() gate — the glm15-21 map — had already proved present-and-string, and that gated site is apply_delta()'s ONLY caller (private on a final class; the glm23-1 split declaration/carrier reunites into the same pipeline; handle_trailing_event never reaches the arms) — the re-checks were dead by construction. The arms append unconditionally now; the per-arm block-type mismatch checks and the input_json_delta partial_json check are untouched. A source pin (ZaiAnthropicResponseMappingTest::testTheGatedDeltaMemberCarriesNoRecheckInsideApplyDelta) holds BOTH halves of the invariant — exactly one `$this->apply_delta(` call site, and no phpcs-spelled `isset( $delta->text )`/`isset( $delta->thinking )` re-check back inside — mutation-tested all three ways (a pasted text re-check, a pasted thinking re-check, and a second caller each fail the pin).
  - **JsonEncodeGuard::must_encode() rides encode(), the one oracle** (glm32-2, the glm30-6 micro class): the guard method hand-copied encode()'s json_encode-false check plus throw in the very class that exists to single-source the raw oracle. It calls self::encode() with the returned encoding discarded now — exception type and message byte-identical BY CONSTRUCTION (verified byte-identical over a 22-class hostile battery: INF/NAN, recursive graphs, invalid UTF-8, resources, 600-deep nesting); every must_encode call site was already a void statement call (EncodabilityNet's segments, both models' guard sites), and both mapping suites' behavioral message pins (14 zai + 12 zai_anthropic assertions) hold.

  - **glm32 verifier-pass decisions** (two independent lenses — correctness + security — over the full round diff; ZERO confirmed defects, every equivalence claim HELD): glm32-1 held over a static only-caller proof (the gate predicate is truth-table-identical to the dropped check on every shape), 27+57 curated frame sequences plus 3000 seeded and 35,000 hostile fuzz interleavings byte-identical pre/post, and a dead-check instrument proving the deleted guards NEVER fired on any reachable input (zero trips across 20k fuzz sequences plus the named battery); glm32-2 byte-identical over the hostile battery (class, message, code), single json_encode per call, all callers already void. Full suite green in both orders (1248 tests, 29898 assertions, 2 skipped — the live-key gates); phpcs/phpstan/conventions clean. Residuals, both below the finding bar: must_encode's exception ORIGIN moved one frame deeper (the must_encode argument frame prints in both pre- and post-round traces, and the surfaces rewrite these exceptions to fixed messages anyway — glm19-2), and the re-check pins are LEXICAL (they catch the phpcs-spelled re-check; a differently-spelled semantic twin would evade — the repo's source-pin idiom, over-approximation direction safe).

- **glm31 round decisions** (fix round over the 10 verified findings of code-review round 31 — /code-review max, ledger-filtered: the review's 13 finder angles produced 31 candidates, 17 verifiers adjudicated, 11 refuted directly by this ledger; the caller also dropped the fork's `$ships_forwarded_values` candidate — the ledger's own Known-deferred GLM9 cut-over-cap item, no new evidence, a re-flag of a user-triaged deferral):
  - **A corrupt-only streamed usage declaration flags the stream** (glm31-1, the round's one cross-channel defect): a PRESENT non-array `usage` member skipped the merge silently, so a stream whose ONLY usage declaration was corrupt completed with zeroed accounting while the byte-equivalent non-streaming body rejects typed (UsageValidator's object rule — the zai lenient flag rescues exactly null, never a scalar). This is NOT glm28-2's "usage guards are untouched" tolerance re-flagged on new derivation: GLM7 #8's letter covers absent/null MEMBERS of a usage object and partial usage OBJECTS, never a present non-object member, and the empirical cross-channel divergence (glm18-4's class) was new evidence. The FIX is state-bounded, not unconditional, precisely because GLM6 #3's pin covers the other half: the member is remembered in BOTH phases (the glm15-14 one-predicate rule) and malformed_event rises at end of stream — after the post-[DONE] gap-fill — ONLY when NO valid usage member merged in either phase. GLM6 #3's pinned shape survives verbatim (a late "usage":"corrupt" after a valid merge stays superseding noise, generation completes) and is now pinned in BOTH orders (corrupt superseded by a later valid member; corrupt resolved by a trailing valid member); the non-streamed scalar verdict is pinned beside them (byte-equivalence both directions). The oracle capture rule is untouched (present AND an array, GLM6 #3).
  - **Fragments over a non-empty start input is corruption, not a merge** (glm31-2): the tool_use consolidation replaced the content_block_start-carried input wholesale with the decoded input_json_delta fragments — silently discarding start-carried arguments on a reporting-success stream, the class the AnthropicSseAggregator docblock itself names ("silently altered tool arguments on a corrupt stream must fail as a parse error"). Vendor-doc settled: the Messages streaming wire carries tool_use arguments as input_json_delta partial_json fragments over an EMPTY placeholder input at content_block_start (platform.claude.com's streaming guide shows every tool_use start with "input":{}), so both-present is nonconforming and ambiguous — flagged through the tool-input channel (glm20-2 precedence), return null. The adjacent boundaries are pinned and untouched: an empty-string fragment accumulates nothing and the start input stands (the pre-round no-op pin), and a start-block input with NO fragments still becomes the call args. A start input with no fragments and no deltas at all is glm28-4's empty-arguments shape — untouched.
  - **The probe CLI's parser rejects unknown shapes and answers help** (glm31-3): getopt() silently DROPS unrecognized options and the GLM8 #7 pre-scan knew only the three exact tokens, so --surfac=anthropic, -surface, a stray positional, and --help ALL fell to the defaults — and with a key present the probe ran the full live, BILLABLE round trip on the default surface reporting PASS (live-reproduced by the review; pre-existing on master, in-scope because this PR rewrote the parser). The fix is a SEQUENTIAL raw-argv scan: every option-led token must be a known long option (--surface/--plan/--region — this CLI's own flags, the same list the missing-value pre-scan walks), single-dash tokens reject (getopt reads them as undeclared SHORT options and drops them too), positionals reject (a value without its flag is the same silent-defaults class), and a space-separated option's VALUE token is consumed never judged (the whitelists own values; the missing-value pre-scan still owns the bare-option shape). --help/-h answers BEFORE anything else with a usage composed from the owners the validation rides (the built surface map for the surface list/default, AbstractPlanRegionSettings::PLANS/REGIONS for the spellings), exit 0, never reaching the key lookup. The surface map is built BEFORE parsing now (behavior-neutral move; the exit-3 no-facts path fires earlier). glm23-3's argv-absent shape unchanged (nothing to scan).
  - **An unreadable subdirectory converts the self-containment walk's abort to a named violation** (glm31-4): the shared walk aborted with an uncaught UnexpectedValueException — fatal, exit 255, under `composer check` AND `bin/inspect-artifact.php` — while the sibling unused-import scan has converted the same abort to a counted FAIL since glm17-17; build.php's catch already held (UnexpectedValueException extends RuntimeException). NOT a glm17-16 re-flag: that round's guard and pins cover the unused-import scan only — the round-31 finding named the asymmetry. The guard lives at the ONE shared walk (plugin-tools, the glm26-6 altitude), not the two call sites the finding named: the abort becomes a named violation with the partial scan kept, so every consumer's failure channel fires automatically (the gate's count, inspect's list, build's refusing RuntimeException). Pinned with the chmod-000 shape (functional opendir probe; glm17-16's root-defeat note — skip where the host opens through it). The inspect CLI's SEPARATE php -l iterator is NOT covered by this decision (out of the round's scope; it iterates a freshly-built zip's extraction).
  - **DebugLogger's read-append-write is CONSCIOUSLY ACCEPTED as racy** (glm31-5): two concurrent PHP-FPM workers with debug logging enabled can interleave get_option→append→update_option and one worker's row is lost (last writer wins with a COMPLETE array — a dropped diagnostic row, never a corrupted one, in an off-by-default 50-row ring buffer of method/redacted-URL/status/duration). The loop's speculative-race bar: the code is PRE-EXISTING with zero diff on the file in this PR (the PR adds a second producer through existing call sites), WordPress's option store carries no row locking and core itself accepts this read-modify-write class for option-backed state, and there is no empirical repro or production loss report. Do NOT add locking machinery without a real loss report; the boundary is documented at log() (the glm15-24 honest-docblock pattern).
  - **The availability classes carry NO KEY_ENV_NAME mirror** (glm31-6): the two mirrors were production-dead (env/constant resolution is AbstractPlanRegionSettings' own ladder — the availability layer never read them), the glm19-10 test-only-alias class. Deleted with glm19-10-style no-redeclaration pins at both consistency sites; the settings class is the one owner. The OTHER four availability identifiers stay (STATE_OPTION/REGION_PENDING_OPTION/KEY_OPTION/REFUSAL_LABEL are read by the availability layer's own code).
  - **action_links() derives the settings page from the registry** (glm31-7): the plugin-row Settings link hand-named PlanRegionSettings::PAGE_SLUG while every other settings-page consumer (zai.php boot(), DebugSettings) rides ZaiSurfaces::settings_classes()[0] — the last settings-page reference outside the registry's reach, and the OLD behavioral pin asserted the slug VALUE (a stranded literal would still pass it). The link derives the owner now and the pin reads the owner's slug the same way the production code does.
  - **The artifact must-ship pin sweeps the source tree** (glm31-8): the hand-enumerated eight-class list was checklist-in-code (a third surface's classes, or any renamed/dropped file outside the eight, could silently miss the zip) — the pin now asserts EVERY connectors/zai/src file ships, with a non-empty-sweep guard against a vacuous root. The non-src literals (asset, uninstall, LICENSE) and the root-php-files pin stay.
  - **JsonShape::is_list() rides the NATIVE array_is_list()** (glm31-9): the SDK polyfills it for the 7.4 floor (files-autoloaded: vendor/composer/autoload_files.php wires src/polyfills.php) AND its own hot paths call it on every request (PromptBuilder — the plugin's own prompt path — and the OpenAI-compatible parse the zai surface extends), so every functional SDK installation provides it; every JsonShape consumer is SDK-dependent code. The hand-rolled range-over-count idiom is deleted and forbidden EVERYWHERE (the GLM8 #13 extraction pin SUPERSEDED at its site per the GLM10 #4 lesson — it used to require exactly one hand-rolled copy in JsonShape.php); a canary pins the harness context loading the function (named failure, never an undefined-function fatal in a generation). phpcs-compat passes with no ignore (PHPCompatibility 9.3 does not flag the call at the 7.4 floor).
  - **Micro decision** (glm31-10): the is_object_shape() docblock's duplicated summary line + stray '/*' opener (a paste artifact) — deleted; no runtime effect.
  - **Refuted/dropped by the round itself** (do not re-derive): the review fork's `$ships_forwarded_values` finding — the ledger's Known-deferred GLM9 cut-over-cap item re-flagged without new evidence (the user's ledger instruction governs: deferred items are closed by a round's scope decision, not re-tracked as findings).
  - **glm31 verifier-pass decisions** (two independent lenses — correctness + security — over the full round diff; ZERO confirmed defects, every fix's equivalence claim HELD): glm31-1 held over a 19-interleaving usage battery (no interleaving flags with a valid usage present — $usage never returns to null after a merge; the corrupt-only-with-no-choices shape rejects through the no-usable-event channel, so no silent-zeroing shape is constructible); glm31-2 held over a 17-shape tool-input battery (only a replayable non-empty stdClass start input can trip the new check — every other start shape already flags at start_block and leaves the EMPTY placeholder the check reads as unset); glm31-3 held under 16 hand-crafted argv shapes plus a 300-shape canary-key fuzz — no disclosure, no bypass to a defaults run, every pre-round accepted invocation still accepted (two cosmetic non-defects ledgered here: a space-form value spelled exactly `-h`/`--help` answers help exit 0 — help-first is the documented design, no billable run possible; bare `--` now rejects where getopt ignored it — consistent with the no-positionals decision); glm31-4's re-indent proven whitespace-only, partials-kept demonstrated creation-order-aware, and the hiding attack (violating files inside a chmod-000 subdir) FAILS CLOSED at all three consumers (the abort is itself an unconditional violation; nothing packages); glm31-9's swap differentially verified over 18 fixed shapes plus 200k randomized key batteries on 8.5 AND 100k on real PHP 7.4 in docker with the polyfill loaded — zero divergences; the whole-diff sweep found NO new runtime channel of any kind. - **glm30 round decisions** (fix round over the 5 surviving findings of code-review round 30 — /code-review max, ledger-filtered: 10 of the 15 raw findings were re-flags of ledgered decisions and dropped with citations; then the 10 verified cleanup findings the round's 15-cap cut were re-emitted and adjudicated — 2 fixed as glm30-6/7, 8 REFUTED, listed below):
  - **setUp() erases every SDK credential a previous test wired** (glm30-1): two leak channels held a wired credential beyond its test — the registry's per-provider authentication map (bindModelDependencies() stamps it onto new model instances; any later registerProvider() re-applies it to the provider statics) and the credential on AbstractProvider's process-wide availability/directory instances (set through the registry call AND the direct setRequestAuthentication() several suites use). The SDK has no unset API; the reset is reflection writes onto the trait's NULLABLE storage ('unwired' is its default), and the per-instance nulling must go through the DECLARING class — reflection does not see the private trait-composed property from a concrete surface class (empirically: hasProperty() false on ZaiProviderAvailability, true on the abstract base that composed the trait). Transporter and memo state stay untouched. Pinned by a polluter/depends pair wiring both channels; mutation-tested (disable the reset → the dependent test fails on the carried credential). Note for future harness docblocks: PHPUnit parses `@depends` ANYWHERE in a docblock, so prose mentioning the annotation in a test's comment produces "invalid @depends annotation" skips (hit and fixed during the round).
  - **The HTTP-error catalog names each surface by its CARD NAME** (glm30-2): safe_http_message()/to_wp_error() take the API display label as a required parameter; each model declares HTTP_API_LABEL on the card-name chain (the settings layer's PROVIDER_LABEL — glm24-2's direction). 'z.ai API' (PROVIDER_LABEL . ' API') keeps the OpenAI surface's messages byte-identical to the old hardcoded wording; the Anthropic surface passes 'z.ai (Anthropic API)' verbatim (no ' API' suffix — the card name already carries one; that asymmetry is the two card names' shape, documented at the constants). The traits the models compose (ThrowsSafeHttpErrors, SafeGenerationBoundary) read the constant via static:: — a composer without it fatals loudly (the GLM6 #12 discipline). The to_wp_error() fallback ("The %s request failed.") is the ONE wording change on the OpenAI surface, on an unpinned path; both surfaces pin that the 429 catalog text names them.
  - **Every non-2xx status outside 3xx/4xx/5xx is RuntimeException** (glm30-3): the vendor ResponseUtil's exact bucketing — a final 1xx informational (the Response DTO admits 100-599) was mislabeled RedirectException → zai_redirect_error by the old <400 fall-through; 6xx+ joins the invalid class too (previously ServerException). The SDK RuntimeException family carries a status-only, label-bearing message onto the CODE_ERROR mapping. Pinned by a status-family battery (100/101/301/307/400/422/500/599; no 401/403 rows — they record the definitive rejection (glm13-6) and would refuse later rows' generations pre-transport).
  - **TranslatableMessageParts owns the translatable-part predicate** (glm30-4): the shared shape (thought-channel drop, function-call/response translation) lives on Support\TranslatableMessageParts; the ONE deliberate divergence — glm28-4's pinned empty-text tolerance — rides a parameter each model passes from its private EMPTY_TEXT_TRANSLATES constant (zai: true, the empty-string text part maps to a wire text entry; zai_anthropic: false, the Messages block rule drops it). Both pinned behaviors survive byte-for-byte; do not "unify" the flag away and do not copy the predicate back into a model.
  - **OPENAI_TOTAL_DERIVATION_MEMBERS is the named, pinned derivation vocabulary** (glm30-5): an absent total derives prompt+completion (GLM12 #6) from the validator's named constant, deliberately NOT computed from OPENAI_MEMBERS — whether a new validation member counts toward the total is a wire-semantics decision at the constant, never array arithmetic (mirroring the Anthropic side's separate INPUT_SIDE_MEMBERS summation vocabulary). A pin asserts the sets agree today, so an OPENAI_MEMBERS change breaks it and forces the constant's conscious revisit; do not replace the pin with runtime derivation.
  - **Micro decisions** (glm30-6/7): wired_credential() owns the wired-ApiKey pair construction effective_key() and effective_for_authentication() shared (the GLM9 #14 extraction had left the mapping duplicated — the GLM5 #11 divergence class); the zai directory's auth-reader closure is ONE factory (wired_authentication_reader()) — a closure factory, NOT a method callable, because the consumers invoke the reader in their own scope and the closure captures the directory's scope so the raw getter (glm26-4) stays callable without widening visibility (glm21-14's discipline ported to the two zai spellings).

The round's first refutation text had these semantics BACKWARDS ("strict compares by value"); verifier-corrected empirically. Removing the trigger would still change the documented release discipline for zero gain — the ledgered design stands); **credential_binding()'s sha256 memo machinery** (glm15-6 stands — the full-tuple-keyed memo IS the pinned memoization-boundary decision, 2-3 derivations per request documented); **the $lenient parameter on UsageValidator::failure_reason()/reject()** (GLM7 #8 stands — the flag is the pinned legacy-semantics carrier, the parameterized-divergence idiom glm30-4 itself used; glm26-7's one-owner composition keeps per-surface arguments as arguments); **the json_fallback_result()/guard_assembled_params() wrappers** (glm14-4/15-7/16-7/16-8 stand — they are the per-surface composition sites, and their call shapes are source-pinned at ZaiRequestMappingTest/ZaiAnthropicRequestMappingTest); **the 63-line BuildsJsonOutputGuidance trait** (glm28-17 + glm25-3's parents-differ precedent stand — 41 of the 63 lines are the decision documentation, and inlining reverses the extraction); **comment-density reduction** (the glm-round references in comments ARE the decision trail this ledger cites — "documented at the pin/site" is the loop's own supersession mechanism (the GLM10 #4 lesson); no convention caps comment density, and stripping the references orphans every ledger citation).

- **glm29 round decisions** (fix round over the 15 findings of code-review round 29 — /code-review max, ledger-filtered, every finding verified before fixing; then a two-lens verifier pass — independent correctness + security agents over the full round diff, verdicts in the round footer):
  - **The net's fall-through ships the RE-ENCODED artifact** (glm29-1): `EncodabilityNet::encode()`'s `return '';` was REACHABLE — jsonSerialize() is user code whose output can diverge between invocations (full-payload oracle fails, attribution walk's member re-encodes succeed), and glm14-4's ride shipped the empty string as the body with generateTextResult() reporting success (reproduced on the shape through the zai chokepoint). Pinned: the diverging double's invocation count (2) and the shipped body's decoded shape. Do not re-flag the second serialization on the failure path — it only runs after a failed first encode.
  - **The zai aggregator's error-event channel is the twin's, in this wire's spelling** (glm29-2): the signal is a PRESENT `error` member on a decodable event object (absent or explicit null keeps the member's absent semantics — the glm28-1 one-check idiom) OR an `event: error` DECLARATION (the twin's GLM7 #4 discipline: the payload's condition cannot un-declare it — a truncated or undecodable error event still counts; the declaration check runs BEFORE any payload judgment, and only 'error' is read — every other event name keeps the historical ignore per GLM7 #18/GLM1 #14). Both phases flag identically (the glm15-14 one-predicate rule), the model checks has_error() FIRST (the twin's position; the GLM8 #5 JSON fallback cannot fire — its live scenario produces no data: field lines), and the wording is the surface's own ('The chat-completions stream contained an error event.'). glm23-6's tolerances SURVIVE and stay pinned: a scalar `data: null` keeps its non-event skip, and a choices-less object WITHOUT an error member keeps its skip — the round's conscious divergence from the twin (which has no choices shape on its wire). Do not re-widen to payload-agnostic error flagging.

"/sub/$x.php"; require $path;`) also rejects. Deliberately over-detects escaped `"\$x"` (pinned as the conservative direction — a visible false violation beats a silent traversal hole). The old `'${'` spelling test is source-pinned absent. Do not re-narrow the predicate to live-interpolation-only (backslash-parity precision): the over-detection is the design.
  - **A present-but-wrong-type tool_use id/name flags at the Anthropic stream channel** (glm29-4, the glm28-2 parity): start_block()'s accumulator ternaries still null non-string values (the corrupt value never merges) but the guard flags malformed_event first — the channel the zai twin uses for the same shapes, replacing the coincidental-downstream-catch (parse_content_block()'s generic identity rejection) the twin's own comment says corruption must not rely on. The glm20-2 flag precedence is untouched (tool-input still outranks malformed-event when both trip).
