- **Falsy unsupported-option values: the wire-INERT keys only** (GLM5-era, re-refuted GLM9 — then SUPERSEDED for the forwarded keys by glm4-4/GLM4 #4, commit 81b04d2): setTopK(0), setWebSearch(false), and the output-\* family — keys the request builder never forwards — are neutral no-ops (AdvertisedOptionGuard's `! empty()` gate). The WIRE_FORWARDED keys (presencePenalty, frequencyPenalty, logprobs, topLogprobs) reject ANY explicitly-set value, falsy neutrals included (the guard's `null !== $value` gate; the SDK parent forwards every non-null value verbatim, so setLogprobs(false) shipped `"logprobs":false`). Do not cite the old line to tolerate setLogprobs(false), and do not flag its typed rejection as a regression (glm37-2 scope-corrected this line after round 37's reviewer burned a verifier on it).
- **System-role coercion unreachable via SDK enum** (GLM9-ref): the SDK MessageRole enum cannot express the flagged input; not triggerable.
- **Empty-arguments strings keep null-args semantics** (glm6-15): an empty string arguments field is not corruption; it denotes a no-argument call.
- **`items: []` with additionalItems sibling keeps tuple semantics** (glm10-v2): the empty array is data, not a missing object schema.
- **Bare `event:` wire format absent in production** (GLM1 #14): live z.ai SSE capture (70 frames) contained zero bare event: lines; the handling exists as wire-robustness, not a vendor-verified feature.
- **Whitespace-then-BOM sniff claim** (GLM6-ref): refuted in that round; the GLM8 #2 canonical-prefix fix later made the layers consistent — flag the layers ONLY if a new divergence appears.
- **Binding race in record_definitive_verdict** (GLM8-verifier-ref): not reachable on real WordPress (per-request option pinning); harness-only artifact.
- **Version bump on sub-PR releases** (GLM3-ref): consciously deferred to M7 release tooling, not a review finding.
- **Unknown content-block types dropped → stop-reason consistency documented** (GLM1 #15, commit 2cf214c): vendor-quirk dependent, not triggerable via own requests; documented in code, not fixed.
- **zai-surface `has_more` forever-degradation** (GLM3 #14-tenant, R15 design): conscious design (R15); degradation path documented. Do not re-flag as a bug; propose as an improvement only with new impact evidence.
- **Big-integer replay split rule** (glm12-8): raw-wire paths (zai arguments string, Anthropic accumulated input_json) accept beyond-int literals the platform decode keeps EXACT (`%.0f`-verified) and reject only true precision loss; decoded-only paths (Anthropic non-streaming input, pre-decoded zai members, the consolidated-payload oracle) keep the CONSERVATIVE walker rejection because post-decode exact and lossy big floats are the same double. Verifier-round addendum: the glm12-12 replay stamp does not survive a `toArray()`/`fromArray()` round trip (the vendor DTOs rebuild `new self`), so an exact-literal call that replays in-process rejects conservatively after array rehydration — the rehydrated call is a caller-built decoded-only value, judged by the conservative half of this same rule; not a parse/replay divergence within either surface. Do not flag the conservative rejection as a false positive, and do not flag the precise acceptance as missing validation.
- **Replay-stamp skip** (glm12-12): outbound replay guards skip the serializing oracle for `ReplayValidatedFunctionCall` instances — the args were validated at inbound acceptance and the SDK DTO is immutable (no setters), so the stamp can never outlive its arguments. Caller-built plain SDK instances keep the full oracle. Do not flag the skip as missed outbound validation.
- **Unrecognized 2xx probe bodies stay inconclusive** (glm12-1): only the models-list shape is VALID and only the `success:false`+definitive-code envelope is INVALID; any other 2xx body must neither report connected nor persist an unproven invalid verdict (core clears keys on false). Non-credential envelope codes (1113 balance) stay inconclusive like their 429 twin. Refined by glm13-2: the models-list shape is the ONE entry rule the parser also rejects through (`ZaiModelListParser::entry_failure_reason()`) — an empty `data` list and an incomplete page (`has_more`) are NOT the shape (inconclusive, superseding the verdict's earlier paginated-body tolerance); the chat filter and plan intersection stay catalog-only.
- **Empty wired credential authenticates nothing** (glm13-1): the SDK registry wires `ZAI_API_KEY=''` verbatim, so the probe treats an empty wired `ApiKeyRequestAuthentication` exactly like none (effective-key fallback), and `record_definitive_verdict()` records NOTHING for a request the empty credential flew — the verdict binding must name the credential that flew (an empty-wire 401 under the ladder/DB binding is the cross-credential poisoning class). Do not re-flag the empty-wire skip as missed validation.
- **Generation routes record definitive rejections** (glm13-6): a 401/403 on either surface's generation route records its invalid verdict through the availability base before the typed throw (request-time endpoint capture; glm13-1 binding discipline) — the same evidence the probe and both discovery routes persist. Status-judging parity with GLM10 #8's one-source decision; do not flag the recording as double-state.
- **Unknown delta on a never-started index is corruption** (glm13-4, supersedes the Codex R14 #3 seed tolerance): the synthesized-seed path is GONE — every delta type on an unstarted index rejects (input_json_delta through the tool-input channel, everything else through the malformed-event channel); unknown deltas on a STARTED block keep their forward-compatible tolerance. Position rejections (duplicate/contiguity, glm13-5) run before content validation in `start_block()`, so a position-rejected frame never latches the tool-input flag.
- **The tool-result response encodability guard stays PRE-mapping** (glm13-11 exception): the SDK parent's message mapping json_encodes the tool-result response and STRING-CASTS the failure, so by the createRequest() chokepoint an unencodable result has already laundered into `"content": "false"` — the whole-payload encodability net can never see it. Its guard stays eager in the typed walk while every other pure-encodability member rides the net with attribution-on-failure. Do not "complete" the restructure by moving it. glm14 extensions, both settled: (glm14-3) the vendor parent has a SECOND pre-encode launder site (outbound tool-call arguments, covered by the replay-guard override) and a conditional DROP (response_format under a non-JSON mime) — the net's documented contract is verbatim-forwarded members only; (glm14-1) the dropped-schema case got its own eager guard scoped to the non-JSON mime, so the schema is checked exactly once under every configuration — do not flag the eager check as a redundant second serialization (under the JSON mime it does not run).
- **glm14 round decisions**:
  - **The net's encode rides as the request body** (glm14-4): guard_assembled_params() returns the encoded string when the request carries a JSON body and createRequest() hands it to the Request as raw `$data` — `getBody()` returns it as-is, `getData()` is null on the ridden request, and the vendor transport no longer re-encodes. Byte-identical wire; do not flag `getData() === null` as lost payload data, and do not re-add a second serialization "for safety".
  - **Both mislabeled-JSON fallbacks pass the marker subclass** (glm14-2): `FixedMessageResponseException` propagates through both surfaces' `json_fallback_result()` (null-out catches), and the zai_anthropic surface's tool_use input rejections (missing input member, non-object input value, non-replayable arguments) are marker exceptions now — byte-identical ResponseExceptions distinguished only by type. Do not flag the marker family on the twin as an inconsistency with GLM12 #3/GLM8 #5, and do not flag the pass-through as violating the "header promised a stream" contract (every non-marker parse failure still returns null and keeps the stream-typed error).
  - **Opaque wiring records nothing** (glm14-5, extends glm13-1): a non-Api-key wired `RequestAuthentication` (third-party `setRequestAuthentication()` callers only — the SDK registry type-gates) makes the probe inconclusive without flying and the recorders record nothing; one credential flies AND binds, or no verdict persists. Do not re-flag the opaque refusal as missed validation or as a regression of configured-pending reporting. glm16-1 made the guard REACHABLE on the zai_anthropic surface (the probe reads the RAW wired instance through the `raw_request_authentication()` base hook — the protocol wrap's foreign-auth RuntimeException previously laundered into the unwired catch, flying the fallback key and persisting its verdict); the flight credential still goes through the surface's one wrap funnel.
  - **The generation-route recorder lives on SafeGenerationBoundary** (glm14-6): one concrete `record_generation_route_rejection()` riding the trait's `credential_gate_availability()`/`gate_authentication()` hooks, satisfying ThrowsSafeHttpErrors' abstract; the endpoint stash rides `capture_generation_endpoint()`. Do not flag the hoist as a trait-abstract conflict or as a behavior change (both surfaces' glm13-6 pins drive it).
  - **The parser's reason→rejection map is the single source** (glm14-7): typed ENTRY_* constants, one ENTRY_REJECTION_MESSAGES map, unmapped reasons throw the lockstep RuntimeException. Do not flag the RuntimeException as a new failure mode for data conditions (only an unmapped constant reaches it).
  - **fixed() delegates to the SDK factory** (glm14-8): the marker's message is `ResponseException::fromInvalidData(...)->getMessage()` — a deliberate double construction on rejection paths only. Do not flag the extra allocation as a hot-path cost.
  - **The uninstall constant-rung and one-decode tests run behaviorally, the constant ISOLATED in a subprocess** (glm14-9/10, glm15-1 supersession): ZaiUninstallTest's constant test defines ZAI_API_KEY in a CHILD PROCESS only (the GLM8 #15 runner pattern) — the glm14 "runs LAST" isolation was a file-ordering accident that --order-by=random broke (12F+2E, reproduced), so the ordering property is GONE by design: the check pipeline's test step runs --order-by=random, and the parent process pins it never defined the constant. The probe's one-decode pin counts getBody() reads through a Response double. Do not flag the define() as test pollution, the subprocess runner as harness bypass, or the randomized pipeline order as instability.
- **Both surfaces resolve the transporter before the guards** (glm13-12): the vendor's FINAL `generateTextResult()` calls `getHttpTransporter()` before `prepareGenerateTextParams()` on the zai surface — guards-first is structurally impossible there, so the zai_anthropic surface's own `generateTextResult()` mirrors the vendor-mandated order and identical misuse yields identical errors. Do not re-flag the order divergence in the other direction.
- **glm15 round decisions**:
  - **The zai_anthropic net mirrors the zai surface's, with the recursion exception** (glm15-5, extends glm13-11/glm14-4 to the second surface): ONE raw encode at the zai_anthropic request build proves encodability (attribution walk on failure, mapping-order first-bad-wins) and rides as the body string. The TOOL SCHEMA's encodability guard stays EAGER — the recursive normalize transform would memory-fatal on a self-referential schema no net can reject — and the transform encodes (tool-result content, output-schema guidance) keep their eager forms because their STRINGS ride the wire. Multi-bad payloads now reject on the typed walk before the net (same split the zai surface accepted in glm13-11); single-bad messages are byte-identical. Do not flag the eager schema guard as a redundant second serialization or the body-string ride as lost payload data. glm16-7: both surfaces' nets and walk segments live on ONE shared owner (`Support\EncodabilityNet`); each surface composes the segments in ITS mapping order (zai names the system instruction first on a multi-bad payload, zai_anthropic names text first — pinned both ways), and the sampling-options segment is zai-ONLY because the twin's eager transforms reject those members pre-net. The one-pass source pins scan the OWNER for the single raw oracle now (documented supersession).
  - **The test harness primes discovery before generation-driving auth tests** (glm15-1 second half): ZaiAnthropicProvider::model() consults the vendor parent's STATICALLY cached directory instance; a process-earlier test that wired authentication onto it leaves discovery able to authenticate and consume a queued mock, while an unwired cached instance throws pre-transport (the old accidental pass). Primed transients make model resolution deterministic under any order. Do not flag the priming as hiding the static SDK cache — the cache is vendor behavior the suite isolates around, not a plugin bug.
  - **Memoization boundary: pure derivations only** (glm15-6): the endpoint value instance per class|plan|region and the binding hash per FULL input tuple (class, source, endpoint, key) memoize; every option/env/state read stays per-consult because retarget-the-next-request is pinned. Do not propose per-request state memos with invalidation hooks — the harness resets options without firing hooks, so any such memo is the order-dependence class glm15-1 purged.
  - **The fallback's one decode and the shared non-streaming pipeline** (glm15-7/11, supersedes GLM7 #9's third-hand-off pin count — documented at the pin): both surfaces' json_fallback gates on the shared decoder's raw view (the object-root oracle), and the zai surface's non-streamed and fallback branches ride one parse_decoded_chat_body(). Do not flag the pair hand-off as redundant decodes.
  - **The trait IS speaking the protocol** (glm15-8): SpeaksAnthropicMessagesProtocol carries the auth wrap (raw-authentication hook per class) and the fallback wrap; the directory's insteadof resolution keeps the SDK trait's raw getter reachable. Do not flag the trait's getRequestAuthentication as a trait-abstract conflict or the hook indirection as drift surface.
  - **The discovery build has one rule** (glm15-22): the parse-side build seeds the map memo through the SAME chat filter (map_from_ids' rule); the newest-first ORDER rides the stash's own sort (superseded by glm21-13: the seed applies the canonical comparator itself — pinned at the seed pin). Do not flag the seed as a second build or the stash as state that can go stale (a non-matching stash falls back to the rebuild, pinned).
  - **Each surface slug has one owner** (glm15-23): the SDK-FREE settings layer's CACHE_SCOPE owns 'zai'/'zai_anthropic'; the SDK-dependent PROVIDER_ID and REFUSAL_LABEL alias it (the settings layer must stay SDK-free loadable — Codex R2 #3 — so the alias direction is fixed). Do not propose aliasing CACHE_SCOPE from the provider.
  - **The self-containment analyzer is token-masked, length-preserving** (glm15-2): code-shape scans match on wp_connectors_mask_string_contents()'s same-length copy and slice the real statement from the code view. The masker state machine (heredoc, interpolated strings, curly depth) is pinned by the string/heredoc fixture test. Do not flag the double view as overhead or the token-based strip (comments→spaces, EXCEPT the terminator byte since glm17-14) as an offset hazard — offsets are preserved BY DESIGN.
- **glm16 round decisions**:
  - **A WELL-FORMED duplicate trailing terminal is inert** (glm16-2, corrected by the glm16-15 verifier round, boundary narrowed glm17-6): a duplicate `message_stop` carrying a well-formed payload after a completed generation is a no-op — well-formed means a decodable OBJECT whose type member is ABSENT or agrees with the declaration; an object whose type member is non-string or contradicts the event field (`data: {"type":false}`, `data: {"type":"ping"}`) still rejects through the same termination-independent declaration checks its pre-termination twin faces, so object-ness alone is not the boundary. The appending-gateway tolerance is the OpenAI twin's own sentinel rule, extended to the Anthropic terminal. Content-bearing trailing events (message_start, content_block_*, message_delta) still reject, and a GARBLED duplicate (decodable non-object payload, `data: []`/`["lost"]`) rejects through consume_frame()'s non-object check, which judges the terminal name post-termination too (glm16-15). Do not flag the well-formed no-op as lost corruption detection, and do not re-widen the no-op to payload-agnostic — the initial glm16-2 form did exactly that and silently lost corruption detection for the one declared trailing name whose pre-termination twin rejects the same shape (empirically confirmed by two verifier angles).
  - **enum is a data-valued schema keyword** (glm16-3, extends GLM8 #6): default/examples/const/enum pass through the empty-object normalization walk verbatim — enum members are the CONSTANTS a value must equal, not schema positions. Do not flag the exemption.
  - **The scalar tool_result JSON-encode convention is deliberate** (glm16-4): every response value ships as its JSON encoding, strings included. A raw string would be protocol-legal but the zai twin's tool-message content is the VENDOR parent's own json_encode (unoverridable), so raw here would present the same tool result differently across surfaces. Pinned; do not flag the artifact quotes as a bug.
  - **The tool-schema memo is identity-keyed and declaration-set-scoped** (glm16-6, completed by the glm16-16 verifier round): SplObjectStorage keyed by declaration identity — identity-keyed like the PHP 8.0+ WeakMap it substitutes for on the 7.4 floor, but STRONG-keyed: the storage itself pins every declaration it holds until a reset releases it, the exact pinning the resets narrated here compensate for (glm17-7 wording fix: the old "WeakMap's semantics" gloss denied that pinning in the same sentence that documented it). Reset on config identity change, strict declaration-list change, OR an empty/absent-list build (the vendor ModelConfig is MUTABLE — a public setFunctionDeclarations() — so an identity-only reset let a same-config batch loop pin every superseded declaration, empirically confirmed by two verifier angles; glm17-1: every reset site also sat behind the params build's NON-empty gate, so the clear-to-empty idiom reset nothing and the cleared window pinned the superseded set — an empty/absent-list build releases the memo, and the at-most-the-current-set bound holds at every request build); rejections never memoize. Do not propose a spl_object_id map (GC id reuse), a real WeakMap swap on the 8+ floor (different lifetime semantics than the resets are tuned against), or an unbounded per-instance memo.
  - **is_object_shape() does not encode** (glm16-9): it rides JsonShape::is_list() — key inspection decides identically to the old re-encode probe on every DECODED input, and no encoder means the GLM7 #10 raw-oracle divergence cannot arise; the GLM7 #10 mechanism pin superseded accordingly. Do not flag the missing raw-oracle comment/encode as a lost guard.
  - **Uncarriable credential material rejects typed** (glm16-13): control characters or commas in a NON-EMPTY key throw the RuntimeException binding-failure family pre-transport (HeadersCollection splits comma values; nothing pre-PSR-7 rejected CRLF); the empty key keeps its glm13-1 semantics. Do not flag the rejection as over-validation of admin-supplied keys — the message is fixed and key-free.
  - **The mislabeled-body fallback scaffold is shared** (glm16-8): `Support\JsonFallbackResult` owns the one decode + object-root gate + glm14-2 marker contract for both surfaces; the zai surface's parse keeps its Response argument. Do not flag the shared helper as a new abstraction layer.
  - **The uninstall surface set rides one registry** (glm16-14): the three class-based sweeps iterate `$zai_connector_surfaces`; the class-free option literals and the broken-install fallback literals stay separate BY DESIGN (they must run with no plugin class loaded). Do not propose folding them into the registry.
- **glm17 round decisions** (fix round over the 13 CONFIRMED findings of code-review round 17, then a 6-lens verifier pass — 11 raw candidates, all confirmed on adjudication, 8 fixed as glm17-14..17, 3 documented below):
  - **The tool-schema memo bound holds at every request build under all three reconfiguration idioms** (glm17-1, the round's one code bug): the compare-based resets (glm16-16) sat behind prepareGenerateTextParams()'s non-empty gate, so setFunctionDeclarations([]) reset NOTHING and the cleared window pinned the superseded set while the config held none. A build with a cleared/absent list releases the memo (release_tool_schema_memo()); the bound is a BUILD-TIME bound — between a mutation and the next build the old set stays pinned because the vendor ModelConfig has no observation hook. Do not re-flag the lazy window, and do not propose eager invalidation hooks (the glm15-6 memoization-boundary decision: the harness resets options without firing hooks).
  - **The unused-import scanner's contract is pinned by fixtures** (glm17-8/9/11/12, tests/UnusedImportScannerTest.php): imports are FOUND on the token-masked view (a column-0 use line inside nowdoc/heredoc/block-comment text is DATA), the mention check is case-insensitive over the RAW source minus exactly the matched statement bytes (any mention counts — prose comments, docblocks, a comment carrying the statement text itself), and glm16-17's CRLF/EOF detection delta is intentional. strip_comments PRESERVES the comment token's line terminator (glm17-14: PHP < 8.0 includes the trailing newline in T_COMMENT, and blanking it un-anchored ^use/m on the composer-pinned 7.4 floor — empirically confirmed in docker php:7.4-cli). The view invariant is LENGTH, not text (a legal mid-statement comment blanks to spaces; glm17-15). Do not re-widen the mention check to code-only, and do not move the scanner off the shared token views.
  - **Requiring bin/check-conventions.php is side-effect-free** (glm17-16): the CLI diagnostics (error_reporting/ini_set) run inside the runner guard only; the unreadable-FILE branch counts a violation loudly (@file_get_contents with the false return owned), and an unreadable SUBDIRECTORY aborts with UnexpectedValueException which the CLI gate converts to a counted FAIL (the iterator cannot step past it). Both pinned: a dangling symlink (the one unreadable shape that fails under root too) and the symlink-to-directory skip (plain directories are never yielded by LEAVES_ONLY — the glm17-12 plain-dir fixture was vacuous, verifier-confirmed).
- **glm18 round decisions** (fix round over the 14 CONFIRMED findings of code-review round 18, then a 6-lens verifier pass — 41 raw candidates, 5 CONFIRMED on adjudication and fixed as glm18-15..19):
  - **The conservative walker's out-of-range bound is the 2^63 FLOAT literal** (glm18-1): comparing \abs($value) > PHP_INT_MAX widens the int constant to the 2^63 double and TIES at the boundary magnitude, so every literal of the ~2048-wide collapse window above PHP_INT_MAX (…809 → the …808 double) passed the decoded-only walker and was stamped replayable — the precision-loss poisoning glm12-8's conservative half exists to refuse, with one surface's two delivery channels giving opposite verdicts. The bound is 9223372036854775808.0 now; the exact 2^63 literal accepts ONLY through the raw-wire precise rule. glm12-8 is unchanged and enforced at the boundary — do not re-flag the exact-literal decoded rejection or the wire-path acceptance.
  - **zai inbound tool-call identity members reject at parse time** (glm18-2): the SDK parent coerces a missing/non-string id or name to NULL ('' passes verbatim), and such a turn is the one the outbound replay guard rejects on EVERY later request — the Codex R9 #3 discipline extended to the zai surface's parse hook. Do not flag the rejection as over-validation.
  - **maxTokens positivity rejects typed on the zai surface** (glm18-3): parity with the twin's validate_request guard; the vendor ModelConfig's own schema declares minimum 1. Do not flag 0/-x rejection as a false positive.
  - **The zai_anthropic directory records the 200-carried envelope through the probe's ONE precedence** (glm18-4 + glm18-15): record_rejection_body_verdict() rides the glm12-1 rule INCLUDING its models-list-first ordering — a HYBRID body (a well-formed models list that also carries success:false + a definitive code) is VALID evidence and never records invalid at the directory (the glm18-4 form answered the same body INVALID at one site and VALID at the other, empirically reproduced). Non-credential codes (1113) stay inconclusive. The zai (OpenAI) directory keeps STATUS-ONLY recording — the envelope shape is live-attested for the Anthropic route; do not re-flag the twin's omission.
  - **The debug log viewer's malformed-entry guard is five SCALAR members** (glm18-5 + glm18-16): isset() alone passes array-valued members and the renderer's (string) casts warn — the fatal class the guard exists to prevent. Malformed entries skip; the well-formed rest renders; an all-malformed list renders no table.
  - **List-root output schemas reject on zai_anthropic** (glm18-6): the glm13-8 rule at parity — JsonShape::is_list() before the encodability guard, on either signal; [] rejects with it.
  - **The self-containment scanner's write-visibility contract** (glm18-7/8 + glm18-17/18/19; pinned by tests/SelfContainmentLoopWritesTest.php and SelfContainmentCompoundWritesTest.php): an include at $offset reads writes from [0, $offset) PLUS every loop construct containing it (do-while spans include the trailing condition; braceless do over-approximates to EOF) PLUS every function-declaration body containing it (re-entrancy: recursion and repeated callback invocation execute later writes before the include's next activation). Compound assignment operators are writes in BOTH write-shape checks; collecting a compound write with its RHS keeps the every-assignment-must-prove rule covering unions; a by-reference alias whose SOURCE is the include variable refuses the plain-variable proof. Regions are OVER-approximated by design (alternative-syntax bodies, unparsable tails, unclosable braces extend to EOF) — a wider region only refuses proofs, never launders one. Do not flag the EOF over-approximation without a real-tree false violation, and do not re-narrow the visibility model.
  - **Known scanner boundaries** (verifier-demonstrated laundering shapes; phpcs-gated or signature-analysis-bound): by-reference CALLEE parameters (a function taking the include variable by reference writes it with no textual write to match — closing it needs callee signature analysis), `global`-binding cross-scope writes (a function-scoped include reading a top-level write placed after the function definition), and function-PARAMETER bindings on the plain-variable path (the caller's argument wins; the map path refuses signatures, the plain path does not model them). goto backward edges and uppercase-keyword loops launder through the scanner itself but phpcs (Generic.PHP.DiscourageGoto, the lowercase-keywords sniff) fails both shapes, so neither can land while `composer phpcs` gates the tree; hostile unbalanced-brace input degrades the span walks to O(loops × length) but @lint owns that class (the glm17 unbalanced-string sibling).
  - **Refactor decisions** (glm18-10..14): one sanitize_enum() serves the plan/region read AND write paths; the discovery cache-id FORMULA lives once on the endpoint base (compose_discovery_cache_id(), parameterized — LSB stays out of the composition, constant VALUES stay settings-owned per glm15-23, and the broken-install fallback rides it, superseding GLM12 #10's inline mirror); messages_url() routes through api_url(); the availability base's mark_region_switch_pending() delegator is DELETED (zero callers — the SDK-free settings owner is the one entry point); gate_authentication() delegates to raw_request_authentication() so the credential gate and the protocol wrap share one body.

- **glm25 round decisions** (fix round over the 11 findings of code-review round 25, high, ledger-filtered — the 25th round, zero correctness findings again, all cleanup/drift-risk; then a two-lens verifier pass: independent security + correctness agents over the full diff, 1 CONFIRMED finding — the round's own glm25-9 stat-guarded head memo served a STALE head for a same-path, same-size rewrite inside one integer-second mtime (back-to-back repro 30/30; latent only — no current consumer rewrites mid-run), excised as glm25-12 — with every equivalence claim HOLDING: a 22,757-sequence SSE prefix-machine differential plus a 60,000-case fuzz (both zero divergences), a two-tree discovery-seed write differential (identical transient name/value/TTL), a refusal-gate decision matrix including the empty ApiKey key through effective_for_authentication()'s '' branch, byte-compared tokenizer views over 62 real files and 12 hostile fixtures, and mutation-tested pins catching every reintroduced-literal and second-writer shape including heredoc-hidden writers):
  - **The positive discovery row has ONE writer family** (glm25-1): `ZaiDiscoveryCache::store_ids()` is the single positive-row store — `cached_ids()`'s own write routes through it — and the availability base's probe seed calls the owner. A tree-scan pin (ZaiModelDirectoryTest::testThePositiveDiscoveryRowHasOneWriterFamily) holds that every `set_transient(` under connectors/zai/src outside ZaiDiscoveryCache.php is the availability base's ONE probe-miss marker write. Do not re-add a second writer of the row (reader-side contract: glm23-7).
  - **The refusal gate and message builder are PRIVATE** (glm25-2): `generation_refusal_reason()` takes the always-narrowed `ApiKeyRequestAuthentication` as a REQUIRED parameter behind `generation_refusal_for_wired_authentication()`; `refusal_message()`'s only caller is `refuse_generation()`. The fixed wording is pinned per surface through the refuse_generation() THROW (zai invalid-verdict in its suite, zai_anthropic region-pending in its) — not cross-class static builder calls — plus a pin that foreign wiring skips the THROWING wrapper. Do not re-widen the public surface or re-add a default-null credential path.
  - **The generation_prompt stash rides Support\StashesGenerationPrompt** (glm25-3, the MemoizesToolLoopVerdicts precedent for the parents-differ shape): both models compose the trait; the assignment stays at each surface's prepareGenerateTextParams(); the failure closure reads `stashed_generation_prompt()`. Both suites pin the composition, the absent hand-synced declaration, and the assignment statement. Do not copy the property back into a model.

Do not propose signature-based compares, and do not widen the gate to object graphs without a compare that is sound under in-place mutation.
  - **Round-21 refactor decisions** (glm21-6/8/9/10/11/13/14, byte-identical-behavior extractions in the glm19-5 direction): the unit-interval rule (temperature/top_p) and the outbound replay block (stamp skip + oracle + throw, with an optional oracle callable interposing the glm21-5 memo) live on RequestShapeGuard and ToolArgsReplayGuard; the outbound replay wording is UNIFIED on 'could not replay tool call arguments' (both surfaces, byte-identical otherwise); UsageValidator::sum_members() is public and serves the zai absent-total derivation; Support\StreamIndex::sound() owns the stream-index value rule for both aggregators (container fetches stay per-caller); ZaiDiscoveryCache::id_maps_to_metadata() owns the metadata-map filter+key rule and the cold-discovery seed applies the canonical comparator itself (the glm15-22 ORDER clause — "rides the stash's own sort" — is superseded by glm21-13; the supersession is pinned at the seed pin); the zai_anthropic directory's discover_model_ids() uses ONE auth-reader closure and ONE rejection helper for its five sites; bin/zai-live-probe.php builds its surface map from ZaiSurfaces (SDK facts keyed by settings class, CLI whitelist and default derived, missing-facts exit 3) with the lockstep pin upgraded to the full-pairing form (superseding glm20-4's substring pins, documented at the pin). Pin relocations from the glm21-18/19 verifier round: the live-smoke source pin lives in ZaiSurfaceLockstepTest (the smoke suite's setUp skips everything without the live key), and the debug-field pin carries its source half (no hardcoded pair; the derivation statement present) — the behavioral half alone was vacuous while the first registry row IS PlanRegionSettings.

- **glm20 round decisions** (fix round over the 12 ledger-filtered findings of code-review round 20, then an 8-lens verifier pass — 2 raw candidates converging on ONE defect, CONFIRMED on adjudication by two independent refuters and fixed as glm20-13):
  - **The float-form exponent bound is the INT-SAFE magnitude width** (glm20-1 + glm20-13, extends glm19-1): float_literal_is_lossy_integer() bounds the exponent string before any (int) cast — the old arithmetic saturated in the wrong direction ((int) of a 19+-digit digit string → PHP_INT_MAX → the sum widens to float → the (int) re-cast → PHP_INT_MIN), inverting the verdict for saturating exponents. The BOUND is load-bearing: only 19+-digit magnitudes clamp (to 18 nines, itself int-exact). A SMALLER bound (glm20-1's first clamp: >3 digits → 9999) corrupts the digit-shift arithmetic for mantissa padding that CANCELS a genuine exponent in [1000, 10^18): the padded exact spellings of 0.1 and 2^100/2^300 flip accept→reject AT THE LIVE WIRE ENTRY POINT (violating glm19-1's pinned exact-accept), and the padded inexact 1e50 spelling flips reject→accept (reopening the raw-wire-vs-decoded divergence glm19-1 closed). Do not re-flag the saturation fix, and do not narrow or widen the width without re-deriving the cancellation cases (all pinned at both the helper and the wire).
  - **Identity/stop-sequence encodability rides the attribution walk; the SHAPE halves stay eager** (glm20-8, extends glm13-11/glm15-5/glm16-7): the JsonEncodeGuard composites are split — reject_tool_call_identity()/reject_tool_result_identity()/reject_misshapen_stop_sequences() keep the null/empty/type rules eagerly with byte-identical messages; two EncodabilityNet segments (guard_prompt_tool_identities, guard_stop_sequences) carry the encodability attributions, composed per surface at the old eager positions (zai: stop, identities, system, sampling, declarations, text; zai_anthropic: identities, stop, text, system, declarations). glm16-7's pins hold plus two new per-surface identity/stop-first pins. Two documented flips: a MISMATCHED tool-result id now names the eager pairing rejection (the old test fixture's isolation trick is gone — a matched pair shares one id, so the walk names the call side), and unencodable identity + non-replayable args on one call part now names the args (the replay guard runs at mapping) — both reject typed pre-transport either way. The glm13-11 tool-result RESPONSE exception stays eager, untouched.
  - **The surface set rides one cross-file owner** (glm20-4, supersedes glm16-14's per-file registry and glm15-13's literal-list pin — documented at the superseded pins): Support\ZaiSurfaces::SURFACES is SDK-free and carries NO slugs (each surface's slug stays its settings class's CACHE_SCOPE, glm15-23's direction). zai.php's boot() and uninstall.php derive from it (uninstall through its owner chain, the constant read guarded by the chain's readiness — the GLM8 #15 broken-install fatal-avoidance path keeps holding); Plugin::PROVIDER_CLASSES and the live probe stay SDK-dependent and are PINNED to the registry by ZaiSurfaceLockstepTest (provider[i]'s PROVIDER_ID must be surface[i]'s CACHE_SCOPE). The uninstall file now states the surface classes ZERO times — stronger than glm16-14's exactly-once. The glm16-14 class-free literals stay separate BY DESIGN, untouched.
  - **The stream's tool-args diagnosis outranks the generic frame error** (glm20-2): has_malformed_tool_input() is checked before has_malformed_event() in the zai_anthropic stream parse; the GLM8 #5 JSON fallback is unaffected (its live scenario — a whole JSON body mislabeled as a stream — produces no tool blocks, so the flag cannot be set there). Combination-pinned.
  - **The aggregator's usage validation is one helper** (glm20-6): validated_usage_view() serves both usage-carrying frames in AnthropicSseAggregator; null return ≡ validator rejection (a non-null return is ALWAYS an array view — the strict rule rejects a JSON-null usage as not-an-object, so the null return is unambiguous). Source-pinned: exactly one failure_reason() call site in the aggregator.
  - **reject_unsupported() probes the ModelConfig object** (glm20-7): toArray() is null-filtered per member, so the getter's null IS the sparse array's absent key; the getter name derives from the key's camelCase (no parallel key→getter map). The former array-only falsy flavors ('0' topK, array() outputFileType) are gone WITH their input class — the DTO's typed setters cannot hold them (the GLM9-ref unreachable-by-construction reasoning).
  - **The unused-import scanner recognizes group-use** (glm20-3, extends glm17-8/9): openings are matched on the same token-masked view; members are unrolled from the MASKED statement bytes — a comma inside a blanked comment cannot hide a member (the fail-open shape the fixture pins); unterminated groups stay NEUTRAL (@lint owns unparseable files, the glm17 boundary). Do not move the member split onto the raw bytes.
  - **One delimiter-parameterized matcher** (glm20-10): wp_connectors_matching_delimiter_end() returns the closing offset or false; the brace wrapper wears the visibility spans' EOF over-approximation policy (the glm18-7 boundary) and the paren wrapper stays honest (false) — the old EOF-vs-false split is a stated per-wrapper POLICY now, not copy drift. Source-pinned to exactly one depth loop.
  - **Harness parity decisions** (glm20-9/11/12): wpdb::prepare() escapes only '$' in preg replacements — the addslashes/replace backslash COLLAPSE get_col()'s LIKE-to-regex conversion relies on is deliberately preserved (escaping the backslash would double every backslash in the harness's SQL); wiredZaiAnthropicModel() is THE zai_anthropic wiring helper (per-suite model() helpers are one-line delegates); current_time('mysql') mirrors the timestamp branch's gmt/offset arithmetic (no plugin caller uses the mysql form — latent-safe).
  - **glm20-5**: both aggregator headers and one inline comment stop claiming the glm19-11-deleted malformed counter (the glm19-13 class, missed at the file headers).

- **glm19 round decisions** (fix round over the 12 findings of code-review round 19, then a 7-lens verifier pass — 11 raw candidates, 2 CONFIRMED on adjudication and fixed as glm19-13/14, 9 refuted with empirical repros and ledger citations):
  - **Float-form spellings join the raw-wire exactness oracle** (glm19-1, extends glm12-8/glm18-1): the plain-integer scan's e/E/./- adjacency guards hid every float token from the rule, so the exponent spelling of a lossy beyond-int integer accepted where its plain twin rejects. A second scan reconstructs each float-form token's exact decimal integer expansion and applies the same %.0f oracle. Do not re-flag: exact float forms still accept (9223372036854775808e0/…808.0/…808e18, 1e20, 1e22, 2^53 spellings); integral-but-inexact big floats (1e23, 1.5e25, the short DBL_MAX spelling 1.7976931348623157e308) REJECT because the literal's exact integer expansion is not the double — true precision loss per the same oracle, and the decoded walker already rejected those doubles (verifier-confirmed both directions); a float-form token's exact-integer guarantee ends at 2^53 (9007199254740993e0 is lossy where the plain literal decodes to an exact int); the conservative decoded half of glm12-8 is untouched.
  - **The malformed-body rewrite covers the vendor InvalidArgumentException family** (glm19-2, extends glm13-7/glm14-2): a USER-role choice message makes the vendor Candidate() constructor throw InvalidArgumentException — now caught with the ResponseException and rewritten to the fixed malformed-payload message on all three zai transports; on the mislabeled-JSON fallback the rewritten rejection is the non-marker family JsonFallbackResult nulls out, keeping the stream-typed error. Every plugin-thrown InvalidArgumentException is outbound (verifier-traced, all 26 sites) and never runs under the parent's parse; the FixedMessageResponseException marker still passes through. Do not re-flag the multi-catch as diagnostic-laundering.
  - **The replay stamp's defensive-path proof is associative-sibling-scoped** (glm19-3 + glm19-14): the caller-built ASSOCIATIVE sub-path (null raw oracle) now proves replayability through the FULL is_replayable() oracle before the GLM12 #12 stamp; the stdClass sub-path under the same branch stamps on the AGGREGATOR's word — every production null-raw caller is the aggregator channel (verifier-traced: 1667/1712/1763), whose input was replay-validated at acceptance precisely/conservatively per glm12-8, and conservative re-validation there would reject the exact big literals the precise rule accepted. The residual latent shape (a caller-built stdClass with a null raw view would stamp unvalidated) is DOCUMENTED at the sub-path and PINNED as deliberate (the boundary test asserts the stdClass channel stamps 1e20, which the conservative walker rejects). Do not propose closing it with is_replayable()/is_replayable_decoded() on the stdClass path.
  - **PreDecodedResponse forwards provenance** (glm19-4): the constructor takes the WHOLE original Response (status, headers, body ride verbatim); the vendor parse reads getData() only, so the forwarding is inert to parsing. Do not re-add a synthetic empty view.
  - **RequestShapeGuard owns the five twin request rules; messages interpolate PROVIDER_LABEL** (glm19-5/6, follows glm15-23's direction): empty/duplicate tool name, list-root parameter schema (non-empty list boundary), list-root output schema ([] rejects), maxTokens-positive live on the shared label-parameterized guard; both surfaces' messages ride the constant chain (REFUSAL_LABEL → CACHE_SCOPE), byte-identical to the old literals. Two same-shape literals remained in ZaiAnthropicRequestAuthentication (out of the round's reviewed scope) — folded by glm24-3 (see the glm24 round; the deferral is closed).
  - **The directory cache alias constants are deleted** (glm19-10, supersedes GLM4 #10's alias-agreement pins — documented here per the GLM10 #4 lesson): the eight production-dead mirrors are gone, the 37 test references ride the real owners (ZaiDiscoveryCache for TTLs/suffix, the settings classes for the prefix), and no-redeclaration anti-regression pins hold both directories. Do not re-add the aliases; do not flag the ZaiDiscoveryCache owner docblock (glm19-13 records the deletion there).
  - **The SSE aggregators' observability API is gone** (glm19-11): is_done()/event_count()/malformed_count() deleted from both aggregators together with the dead-store counters (zai $malformed; twin $events/$malformed); the load-bearing fields stay (zai $done/$event_count gate aggregated(); the twin's $terminated gates the frames). Termination/event pins read state via the harness reflection helper (aggregator_state()); malformed-count pins became behavioral assertions. Do not re-add public observability API to the aggregators.
  - **Plugin::PROVIDER_ID is deleted** (glm19-12): the docblock-advertised constant orphaned by the PROVIDER_CLASSES rewire; the provider classes' metadata is the registry's ID source.
  - **Verifier-round fixes** (glm19-13/14): docblocks claiming deleted behavior rewritten (the DISCOVERY_TTL present-tense alias claim, the absint()/event_count() narration drift); the stdClass trust boundary stated and pinned (see glm19-3+14 above).

## Verification-protocol decisions

- **Verification verdicts come from transcripts, not subagent vote tallies** (GLM1 lesson): the 12/3 CONFIRMED/PLAUSIBLE tally was corrected from a 9/2/4 vote read.
- **Empiricism over introspection** (GLM1 #14 lesson): wire-format claims are settled by live capture (curl), not model self-knowledge.
- **Test pins may be consciously superseded** (GLM10 #4 lesson): when an invariant has been extended twice (glm9-16), superseding the older pin is correct — document the supersession in the CHANGELOG.

## Drift-guard scope (post-GLM11)

- The label drift guard covers the model + discovery paths (ZaiModelListParser, both metadata directories, availability base). The **endpoint layer deliberately brands UNKNOWN_ENDPOINT_LABEL with 'z.ai'/'z.ai Anthropic'** (ZaiEndpoint.php:123, ZaiAnthropicEndpoint.php:150) — that is product naming, not drift; do not flag.

## Known deferred items (improvements, not bugs)

- **glm17 verifier-round boundaries (all pre-existing, none reachable in the repo today, verifier-confirmed)**:
  - **Inline-HTML column-0 use lines stay unmasked**: wp_connectors_mask_string_contents() passes T_INLINE_HTML verbatim, so a column-0 `use ...;` line in template HTML after `?>` still phantom-flags (identical behavior in the pre-glm17-8 raw-regex scanner — not a regression). 0 of 50 connectors/ files contain a close tag, and masking inline HTML in the SHARED masker would change the glm15-2 analyzer's views — a design decision, not a scanner patch. Re-open only when a connectors/ file legitimately carries template HTML.
  - **A column-0 trait-use statement phantom-flags as an unused import** (`use SomeTrait;` at column 0 inside a class body): pre-existing raw-regex behavior, and phpcs WordPress-Core rejects unindented class-body statements, so the shape cannot land while `composer phpcs` gates the tree. Re-open only if the phpcs gate is dropped.
  - **An unbalanced string delimiter collapses the masked view's tail to spaces** (a botched merge leaves a bare quote; every use statement after it is invisible to the masked scan and the STANDALONE `composer conventions` run stays green): the full `composer check` pipeline runs `@lint` (php -l over all sources) BEFORE `@conventions`, which owns exactly this failure class. Do not add an in-scanner parseability probe for it — that duplicates the lint step's job.
- **SseFrameBuffer::feed() incremental-scan cursor deferred** (glm15-24, a PLAUSIBLE latent finding accepted with its bound documented): each feed() re-normalizes and rescans the ENTIRE unconsumed buffer, so incremental chunk feeding is O(k · tail) — quadratic in the unconsumed tail, never in the whole stream. LATENT: both production callers (both model parsers) feed the complete body in ONE call. A persistent normalized/scanned cursor is the fix shape; deferred because the prefix-strip states rewrite the buffer head and the CR-hold rewrites its tail (every cursor transition is regression surface in the most heavily pinned support class) for zero current benefit. Do not re-flag the latent bound without an incremental consumer or a demonstrated slow path; the docblock at feed() is the honest statement.
- GLM2's 5 minor cleanup items + latent #8 (duplicate settings errors masked by wp_die) — deferred by user triage.
- GLM8-perf trio (negative-caching conflict with M1 decision; /models parsing duplication was since fixed in glm1-13) — superseded or deferred.
- GLM9 cut-over-cap items: credential/hook/render twins, is_object_shape() vs JsonShape::is_list() (unified glm9; the zai_anthropic copy joined it in glm16-9), triple property_exists, usage-oracle duplication (since unified), whole-payload encode oracle on zai streamed path (since swapped glm10-10), per-call chat-ID set rebuild, ~15 source-pinning tests, $ships_forwarded_values hand-flag, live probe's nine surface ternaries (since table-ized glm10-15).

Ledger maintained by the review loop; update when a decision changes, not when a reviewer disagrees.
